#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The original is a single naïve CPU loop. The hybrid design I’m applying is: MPI partitions output rows across ranks, OpenMP parallelizes deterministic host initialization and validation, and each rank runs its local dense product through CUDA’s tuned cuBLAS DGEMM. Only rank 0 gathers the full result, so communication and memory scale cleanly while preserving `-v` and `-r` behavior.

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

+                fail("MPI_Gatherv", "matrix displacement exceeds MPI int range", rank);

-

+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, localA.data(), localA.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copy A to GPU", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, b.data(), b.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copy B to GPU", rank);
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        // Row-major C=A*B is column-major C^T=B^T*A^T in the same storage.
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), &alpha, deviceB,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), deviceA, static_cast<int>(n),
+                                &beta, deviceC, static_cast<int>(n)),
+                    "cublasDgemm", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC.data(), deviceC, localC.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copy C from GPU", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "cudaDeviceSynchronize", rank);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? c.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(b.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(B)", rank);
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localA.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localC.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(C)", rank);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)", rank);
+
+    int result = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const auto milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0));
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(n);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", operations / elapsed / 1.0e9);
+        if (printResults) print_results(c, "MatrixC");
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(b, c, n);
+            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+            result = valid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return result;
 }

+void fail(const char* operation, const char* detail, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
+    }

+
+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));
+        fail(operation, message, rank);
+    }

+
+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
 }

+    double* deviceB = nullptr;
+    double* deviceC = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB), b.size() * sizeof(double)),
+              "cudaMalloc(B)", rank);
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA), localA.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                  "cudaMalloc(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC), localC.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                  "cudaMalloc(C)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(localA.data(), localA.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaHostRegisterDefault),
+                  "cudaHostRegister(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(localC.data(), localC.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaHostRegisterDefault),
+                  "cudaHostRegister(C)", rank);
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(b.data(), b.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterDefault),
+              "cudaHostRegister(B)", rank);
+
+    cublasHandle_t handle;
+    checkCublas(cublasCreate(&handle), "cublasCreate", rank);
+    checkCublas(cublasSetMathMode(handle, CUBLAS_DEFAULT_MATH), "cublasSetMathMode", rank);
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        receiveCounts.resize(ranks);
+        displacements.resize(ranks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+            const size_t rows = rowsForRank(n, r, ranks);
+            const size_t offset = firstRowForRank(n, r, ranks) * n;
+            if (offset > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+                fail("MPI_Gatherv", "matrix displacement exceeds MPI int range", rank);
+            }
+            receiveCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * n);
+            displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    if (rank == 0) std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, localA.data(), localA.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copy A to GPU", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, b.data(), b.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copy B to GPU", rank);
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        // Row-major C=A*B is column-major C^T=B^T*A^T in the same storage.
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), &alpha, deviceB,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), deviceA, static_cast<int>(n),
+                                &beta, deviceC, static_cast<int>(n)),
+                    "cublasDgemm", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC.data(), deviceC, localC.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copy C from GPU", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "cudaDeviceSynchronize", rank);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? c.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(b.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(B)", rank);
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localA.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localC.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(C)", rank);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)", rank);
+
+    int result = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const auto milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0));
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(n);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", operations / elapsed / 1.0e9);
+        if (printResults) print_results(c, "MatrixC");
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(b, c, n);
+            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+            result = valid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return result;
 }

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {

+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
 }

+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(b.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(B)", rank);
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localA.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localC.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(C)", rank);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)", rank);
+
+    int result = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const auto milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0));
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(n);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", operations / elapsed / 1.0e9);
+        if (printResults) print_results(c, "MatrixC");
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(b, c, n);
+            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+            result = valid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
+        }
+    }
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return result;
 }

+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);

+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);

+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);

+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);

+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));
+        fail(operation, message, rank);
+    }

+
+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+}
+
+void initLocalA(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
+                size_t localRows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)

+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
+    }

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));
+        fail(operation, message, rank);

+
+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+}
+
+void initLocalA(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
+                size_t localRows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localRows; ++localI) {
+        const size_t globalI = firstRow + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            a[localI * n + j] = getPseudoRndValue(n, globalI, j);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
+    }
+}

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));
+        fail(operation, message, rank);
+    }
+}
+
+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+}
+
+void initLocalA(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
+                size_t localRows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localRows; ++localI) {
+        const size_t globalI = firstRow + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            a[localI * n + j] = getPseudoRndValue(n, globalI, j);

     }

+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), static_cast<int>(localRows),
+                                static_cast<int>(n), &alpha, deviceB,
+                                static_cast<int>(n), deviceA, static_cast<int>(n),
+                                &beta, deviceC, static_cast<int>(n)),
+                    "cublasDgemm", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC.data(), deviceC, localC.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "copy C from GPU", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "cudaDeviceSynchronize", rank);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? c.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(b.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(B)", rank);
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localA.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localC.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(C)", rank);
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)", rank);
+
+    int result = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const auto milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0));
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(n);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", operations / elapsed / 1.0e9);
+        if (printResults) print_results(c, "MatrixC");
+        if (validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(b, c, n);
+            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+            result = valid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return result;
 }

codex
The hybrid binary now builds cleanly, runs with two MPI ranks, uses OpenMP during host work, maps ranks to GPUs, and passes validation (including gathered result output). One performance issue showed up in the timing: the first cuBLAS call pays CUDA’s lazy kernel-loading cost, so I’m moving that one-time initialization into an untimed warm-up to make the reported benchmark reflect steady-state matrix multiplication.
apply patch

+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+}

+void initLocalA(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
+                size_t localRows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localRows; ++localI) {
+        const size_t globalI = firstRow + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            a[localI * n + j] = getPseudoRndValue(n, globalI, j);

+void fail(const char* operation, const char* detail, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
+    }

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {

+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));

+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);

+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+}
+
+void initLocalA(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
+                size_t localRows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localRows; ++localI) {
+        const size_t globalI = firstRow + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            a[localI * n + j] = getPseudoRndValue(n, globalI, j);
         }
     }
 }

+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+}
+
+void initLocalA(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
+                size_t localRows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localRows; ++localI) {
+        const size_t globalI = firstRow + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            a[localI * n + j] = getPseudoRndValue(n, globalI, j);
         }
     }

+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {

+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));

    15	namespace {
    16	
    17	void fail(const char* operation, const char* detail, int rank) {
    18	    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);
    19	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    20	    std::abort();
    21	}
    22	
    23	void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
    24	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
    25	        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
    26	    }
    27	}
    28	
    29	void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
    30	    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
    31	        char message[64];
    32	        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));
    33	        fail(operation, message, rank);
    34	    }
    35	}
    36	
    37	// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
    38	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
    39	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
    40	           static_cast<double>(n * n);
    41	}
    42	
    43	size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
    44	    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
    45	    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
    46	    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
    47	           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
    48	}
    49	
    50	size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
    51	    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
    52	           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
    53	}
    54	
    55	void initLocalA(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
    56	                size_t localRows) {
    57	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    58	    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localRows; ++localI) {
    59	        const size_t globalI = firstRow + localI;
    60	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
    61	            a[localI * n + j] = getPseudoRndValue(n, globalI, j);
    62	        }
    63	    }
    64	}
    65	
    66	void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& matrix, size_t n) {
    67	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    68	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
    69	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
    70	            matrix[i * n + j] = getPseudoRndValue(n, i, j);
    71	        }
    72	    }
    73	}
    74	
    75	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& b, const std::vector<double>& c,
    76	                    size_t n) {
    77	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    78	    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
    79	        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
    80	            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % n;
    81	            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % n;
    82	            double expected = 0.0;
    83	            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
    84	                expected += getPseudoRndValue(n, i, k) * b[k * n + j];
    85	            }
    86	            const double actual = c[i * n + j];
    87	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
    88	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
    89	                std::printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, "
    90	                            "got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
    91	                            i, j, expected, actual, relError);
    92	                return false;
    93	            }
    94	        }
    95	    }
    96	    return true;
    97	}
    98

   100	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program);

   104	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   105	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   106	}

   225	        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N, 1, 1, 1,
   226	                                &alpha, deviceB, 1, deviceA, 1, &beta, deviceC, 1),
   227	                    "cuBLAS warm-up", rank);
   228	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "CUDA warm-up", rank);
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
   232	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   233	    if (rank == 0) {
   234	        receiveCounts.resize(ranks);
   235	        displacements.resize(ranks);
   236	        for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
   237	            const size_t rows = rowsForRank(n, r, ranks);
   238	            const size_t offset = firstRowForRank(n, r, ranks) * n;
   239	            if (offset > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   240	                fail("MPI_Gatherv", "matrix displacement exceeds MPI int range", rank);
   241	            }
   242	            receiveCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * n);
   243	            displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);
   244	        }
   245	    }
   246	
   247	    if (rank == 0) std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   248	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   249	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   250	
   251	    if (localRows != 0) {
   252	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceA, localA.data(), localA.size() * sizeof(double),
   253	                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   254	                  "copy A to GPU", rank);
   255	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(deviceB, b.data(), b.size() * sizeof(double),
   256	                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   257	                  "copy B to GPU", rank);
   258	        const double alpha = 1.0;
   259	        const double beta = 0.0;
   260	        // Row-major C=A*B is column-major C^T=B^T*A^T in the same storage.
   261	        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
   262	                                static_cast<int>(n), static_cast<int>(localRows),
   263	                                static_cast<int>(n), &alpha, deviceB,
   264	                                static_cast<int>(n), deviceA, static_cast<int>(n),
   265	                                &beta, deviceC, static_cast<int>(n)),
   266	                    "cublasDgemm", rank);
   267	        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC.data(), deviceC, localC.size() * sizeof(double),
   268	                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
   269	                  "copy C from GPU", rank);
   270	        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "cudaDeviceSynchronize", rank);
   271	    }
   272	
   273	    MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
   274	                rank == 0 ? c.data() : nullptr,
   275	                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
   276	                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
   277	                MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   278	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   279	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   280	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   281	
   282	    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy", rank);
   283	    checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(b.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(B)", rank);
   284	    if (localRows != 0) {
   285	        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localA.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(A)", rank);
   286	        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localC.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(C)", rank);
   287	    }
   288	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)", rank);
   289	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)", rank);
   290	    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)", rank);
   291	
   292	    int result = EXIT_SUCCESS;
   293	    if (rank == 0) {
   294	        const auto milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0));
   295	        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
   296	                                  static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(n);
   297	        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
   298	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", operations / elapsed / 1.0e9);
   299	        if (printResults) print_results(c, "MatrixC");
   300	        if (validate) {
   301	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   302	            const bool valid = validateResult(b, c, n);
   303	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   304	            result = valid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
   305	        }
   306	    }
   307	
   308	    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   309	    MPI_Finalize();
   310	    return result;
   311	}
     1	cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
     2	project(matmul_benchmark LANGUAGES CXX CUDA)
     3	
     4	find_package(MPI REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
     5	find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)
     6	find_package(CUDAToolkit REQUIRED)
     7	
     8	set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
     9	set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
    10	set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD 20)
    11	set(CMAKE_CUDA_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
    12	
    13	# Compile the existing source with nvcc so CUDA and cuBLAS are always active.
    14	set_source_files_properties(matmul.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)
    15	add_executable(matmul matmul.cpp)
    16

    18	    CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native
    19	    CUDA_SEPARABLE_COMPILATION OFF
    20	)
    21	
    22	target_compile_options(matmul PRIVATE
    23	    $<$<COMPILE_LANGUAGE:CUDA>:-O3;-Xcompiler=-O3,-march=native,-Wall,-Wextra,-fopenmp>
    24	)
    25

    27	    MPI::MPI_CXX
    28	    OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX
    29	    CUDA::cudart
    30	    CUDA::cublas
    31	    m
    32	)

+void fail(const char* operation, const char* detail, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {

+    }

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {

+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));

+           static_cast<double>(n * n);

+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
+}
+
+void initLocalA(std::vector<double>& a, size_t n, size_t firstRow,
+                size_t localRows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < localRows; ++localI) {
+        const size_t globalI = firstRow + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            a[localI * n + j] = getPseudoRndValue(n, globalI, j);
         }
     }
 }

+    MPI_Gatherv(localC.data(), static_cast<int>(localC.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                rank == 0 ? c.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? receiveCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+    checkCublas(cublasDestroy(handle), "cublasDestroy", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(b.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(B)", rank);
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localA.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostUnregister(localC.data()), "cudaHostUnregister(C)", rank);
     }

-

-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceA), "cudaFree(A)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceB), "cudaFree(B)", rank);
+    checkCuda(cudaFree(deviceC), "cudaFree(C)", rank);
+
+    int result = EXIT_SUCCESS;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const auto milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(std::llround(elapsed * 1000.0));
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(n) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(n) * static_cast<double>(n);
+        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", operations / elapsed / 1.0e9);
+        if (printResults) print_results(c, "MatrixC");

+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return result;

+void fail(const char* operation, const char* detail, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
+    }
+}

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));
+        fail(operation, message, rank);

+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
 }

+void fail(const char* operation, const char* detail, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
+    }

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[64];

+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));

+void fail(const char* operation, const char* detail, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));
+        fail(operation, message, rank);
+    }
+}
+
+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
 }

codex
Implemented the hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA benchmark while preserving the `matmul` executable and existing CLI.

Key changes:

- MPI row-wise workload distribution and result gathering.
- OpenMP-parallel matrix initialization.
- CUDA/cuBLAS DGEMM on each rank’s assigned GPU.
- Node-local MPI rank-to-GPU mapping.
- Pinned host memory and asynchronous GPU transfers.
- Untimed CUDA/cuBLAS warm-up.
- MPI-wide timing and aggregate GFLOPS reporting.
- Preserved validation and result-output behavior.
- Updated CMake for mandatory MPI, OpenMP, CUDA, and cuBLAS.

Files changed:

- [matmul.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r2/matmul/matmul.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/matmul_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r2/matmul/CMakeLists.txt)

Verification succeeded:

```text

2 OpenMP threads/rank
CUDA/cuBLAS linked and active

```

No files were added, and the executable remains `matmul`.

+namespace {
+
+void fail(const char* operation, const char* detail, int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: %s failed: %s\n", rank, operation, detail);
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fail(operation, cudaGetErrorString(status), rank);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation, int rank) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        char message[64];
+        std::snprintf(message, sizeof(message), "cuBLAS status %d", static_cast<int>(status));
+        fail(operation, message, rank);
+    }
+}
+
+// Generate the same deterministic values as the original benchmark.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(size_t n, size_t i, size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (n * n)) /
+           static_cast<double>(n * n);
+}
+
+size_t firstRowForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    const size_t base = n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    return static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+           std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), extra);
+}
+
+size_t rowsForRank(size_t n, int rank, int ranks) {
+    return n / static_cast<size_t>(ranks) +
+           (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < n % static_cast<size_t>(ranks));
 }

+    const size_t localRows = rowsForRank(n, rank, ranks);
+    const size_t firstRow = firstRowForRank(n, rank, ranks);
+    if (localRows != 0 && n > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) / localRows) {
+        fail("MPI_Gatherv", "per-rank matrix partition exceeds MPI int count", rank);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Comm nodeComm;
+    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
+                        &nodeComm);
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &localRank);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);
+
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), "cudaGetDeviceCount", rank);
+    if (deviceCount == 0) fail("CUDA setup", "no CUDA device is available", rank);
+    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
+    checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device), "cudaSetDevice", rank);

+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties", rank);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {

+        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", n, n);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("Parallel configuration: %d MPI rank(s), up to %d OpenMP "
+                    "thread(s)/rank, CUDA/cuBLAS\n",
+                    ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Rank 0 CUDA device: %s\n", properties.name);
+        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> localA(localRows * n);
+    std::vector<double> b(n * n);
+    std::vector<double> localC(localRows * n);
+    std::vector<double> c;
+    if (rank == 0) c.resize(n * n);
+    initLocalA(localA, n, firstRow, localRows);
+    initMatrix(b, n);
+
+    double* deviceA = nullptr;
+    double* deviceB = nullptr;
+    double* deviceC = nullptr;
+    checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceB), b.size() * sizeof(double)),
+              "cudaMalloc(B)", rank);
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceA), localA.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                  "cudaMalloc(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&deviceC), localC.size() * sizeof(double)),
+                  "cudaMalloc(C)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(localA.data(), localA.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaHostRegisterDefault),
+                  "cudaHostRegister(A)", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(localC.data(), localC.size() * sizeof(double),
+                                   cudaHostRegisterDefault),
+                  "cudaHostRegister(C)", rank);
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaHostRegister(b.data(), b.size() * sizeof(double), cudaHostRegisterDefault),
+              "cudaHostRegister(B)", rank);
+
+    cublasHandle_t handle;
+    checkCublas(cublasCreate(&handle), "cublasCreate", rank);
+    checkCublas(cublasSetMathMode(handle, CUBLAS_DEFAULT_MATH), "cublasSetMathMode", rank);
+
+    // Force CUDA context and cuBLAS kernel initialization out of the measured region.
+    if (localRows != 0) {
+        const double alpha = 1.0;
+        const double beta = 0.0;
+        checkCublas(cublasDgemm(handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N, 1, 1, 1,
+                                &alpha, deviceB, 1, deviceA, 1, &beta, deviceC, 1),
+                    "cuBLAS warm-up", rank);
+        checkCuda(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "CUDA warm-up", rank);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<int> receiveCounts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        receiveCounts.resize(ranks);
+        displacements.resize(ranks);
+        for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+            const size_t rows = rowsForRank(n, r, ranks);
+            const size_t offset = firstRowForRank(n, r, ranks) * n;
+            if (offset > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+                fail("MPI_Gatherv", "matrix displacement exceeds MPI int range", rank);
+            }
+            receiveCounts[r] = static_cast<int>(rows * n);
+            displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(offset);
         }

+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+                                  cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),

Key changes:

- MPI row-wise workload distribution and result gathering.
- OpenMP-parallel matrix initialization.
- CUDA/cuBLAS DGEMM on each rank’s assigned GPU.
- Node-local MPI rank-to-GPU mapping.
- Pinned host memory and asynchronous GPU transfers.
- Untimed CUDA/cuBLAS warm-up.
- MPI-wide timing and aggregate GFLOPS reporting.
- Preserved validation and result-output behavior.
- Updated CMake for mandatory MPI, OpenMP, CUDA, and cuBLAS.
